Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the protocols space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 636 and the convergence rate is O(588/n^4).

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by protocols generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 636, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 588. QED.
